pvsim/scenarios.py

"""Scenario factories: modify geometry and initial conditions, not the simulator."""
from dataclasses import replace
import json
import random
from pathlib import Path
from .domain import AgentSpec, AgentState, Road, Settings, Scenario


def corridor(width=3.2, pedestrians=3, vehicles=2, seed=0, settings=None,
             vehicle_speed=3.0, pedestrian_speed=1.3, lateral_speed=0.7,
             vehicle_lateral_speed=0.0, centered_vehicle=False,
             pedestrian_spacing=2.5, vehicle_spacing=9.0, speed_jitter=0.08,
             pedestrian_width=0.6, vehicle_width=1.8, initial_gap=28.0,
             mass_scale=1.0, bottleneck=False):
    c = settings or Settings()
    if width <= 0 or pedestrians < 1 or vehicles < 1:
        raise ValueError("Need positive width and at least one of each agent")
    road = Road(60.0, ((0.0, width), (60.0, width)))
    if bottleneck:
        road = Road(60.0, ((0.0, 5.0), (12.0, 5.0), (18.0, width),
                           (42.0, width), (48.0, 5.0), (60.0, 5.0)))
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    lane_y = 0.0 if centered_vehicle else -width / 2 + c.wall_clearance + vehicle_width / 2
    agents = []
    for i in range(vehicles):
        speed = vehicle_speed * (1 + rng.uniform(-speed_jitter, speed_jitter))
        p = AgentSpec(f"V{i+1}", "vehicle", 4.5, vehicle_width, speed, 1.5, 3.0,
                      vehicle_lateral_speed, 0.4, 1, -1, lane_y, 1500 * mass_scale,
                      encounter_speed=1.0)
        agents.append(AgentState(p, 16.0 - i * vehicle_spacing, lane_y, speed))
    for i in range(pedestrians):
        speed = pedestrian_speed * (1 + rng.uniform(-speed_jitter, speed_jitter))
        p = AgentSpec(f"P{i+1}", "pedestrian", 0.5, pedestrian_width, speed, 1.2, 2.0,
                      lateral_speed, 1.0, -1, 1, lane_y, 70 * mass_scale,
                      encounter_speed=1.0)
        agents.append(AgentState(p, 16.0 + initial_gap + i * pedestrian_spacing, lane_y, speed))
    return Scenario("bottleneck" if bottleneck else "corridor", road, agents, c, seed,
                    {"width": width, "centered_vehicle": centered_vehicle,
                     "vehicle_lateral_speed": vehicle_lateral_speed,
                     "initial_gap": initial_gap, "speed_jitter": speed_jitter})


def load_scenario(path):
    data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    settings = Settings(**data.get("settings", {}))
    if "agents" not in data:
        factory = ring if data.get("type") in ("ring", "circle_crossing") else corridor
        return factory(settings=settings, **data.get("scenario", {}))
    road = Road(data["road"]["length"], tuple(tuple(p) for p in data["road"]["profile"]), data["road"].get("periodic", False))
    agents = []
    for row in data["agents"]:
        p = AgentSpec(**row["spec"])
        agents.append(AgentState(p, **row["state"]))
    return Scenario(data.get("name", "custom"), road, agents, settings, data.get("seed", 0))


def periodic_corridor(width=3.2, circumference=80.0, duration=180.0, settings=None, **kwargs):
    if circumference < 60:
        raise ValueError("Default initial layout needs circumference >=60 m")
    c = replace(settings or Settings(), max_time=duration)
    scenario = corridor(width=width, settings=c, **kwargs)
    scenario.road = Road(circumference, ((0.0, width), (circumference, width)), periodic=True)
    scenario.name = "ring"
    scenario.metadata.update({"topology": "periodic_unrolled_strip", "circumference_m": circumference,
                              "duration_s": duration, "rigid_body_curvature": False})
    return scenario


def ring(**kwargs):
    """Alternating agents on a circle crossing to antipodal destinations."""
    from .radial import circle_crossing
    return circle_crossing(**kwargs)

pvsim/simulation.py

"""Synchronous simulator, independent from the policy, scenario factories and UI."""
from dataclasses import asdict, replace
from itertools import combinations
import copy
import math
from .geometry import swept_contact, swept_separation, swept_wall_gap, wall_gap, separation
from .policy import ReciprocalPolicy
from .diagnostics import feasibility, classify_stall
from .topology import pair_images, relative_front


def snapshot(t, agents):
    return {"time": round(t, 6), "agents": [
        {"id": a.spec.id, "x": round(a.x, 6), "y": round(a.y, 6),
         "speed": round(a.speed, 6), "vy": round(a.vy, 6), "mode": a.mode, "done": a.done}
        for a in agents]}


def validate(scenario):
    if not scenario.agents:
        raise ValueError("Scenario must contain agents")
    ids = [a.spec.id for a in scenario.agents]
    if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
        raise ValueError("Agent ids must be unique")
    for a in scenario.agents:
        if not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (a.x, a.y, a.speed, a.vy, a.spec.home_y)) or a.speed < 0:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid initial state: {a.spec.id}")
        if wall_gap(scenario.road, a) < -1e-9:
            raise ValueError(f"Initial footprint leaves road: {a.spec.id}")
        if not scenario.road.periodic and (a.x - a.spec.length / 2 < 0 or a.x + a.spec.length / 2 > scenario.road.length):
            raise ValueError(f"Initial footprint outside road ends: {a.spec.id}; use fewer agents or custom positions")
        if scenario.road.periodic and (a.spec.length >= scenario.road.length / 2 or
                                      max(a.spec.desired_speed, a.speed) * scenario.settings.dt >= scenario.road.length / 4):
            raise ValueError("Periodic footprints/steps must be small relative to circumference")
    for a, b in combinations(scenario.agents, 2):
        if any(swept_contact(a, a, bi, bi) is not None for bi, _ in pair_images(a, b, b, scenario.road)):
            raise ValueError(f"Initial contact/overlap: {a.spec.id}, {b.spec.id}")


def simulate(scenario, policy=None, record=True):
    from .radial import RadialScenario, simulate_circle
    if isinstance(scenario, RadialScenario):
        return simulate_circle(scenario, policy, record)
    validate(scenario)
    policy = policy or ReciprocalPolicy()
    agents = copy.deepcopy(scenario.agents)
    c, road = scenario.settings, scenario.road
    trace = [snapshot(0.0, agents)] if record else []
    status, events, t, quiet_time = "timeout", [], 0.0, 0.0
    min_gap = min((separation(a, bi) for a, b in combinations(agents, 2)
                   for bi, _ in pair_images(a, b, b, road)), default=float("inf"))
    min_wall = min(wall_gap(road, a) for a in agents)
    collisions, violations, braking_infeasible_steps = set(), set(), 0
    exit_times = {}
    detector_crossings = {a.spec.id: 0 for a in agents}
    pair_passages = 0
    max_steps = math.ceil(c.max_time / c.dt)
    for step in range(1, max_steps + 1):
        dt = min(c.dt, c.max_time - t)
        if dt <= 1e-12:
            break
        step_settings = replace(c, dt=dt)
        # No control sees another agent's already-updated position.
        controls = {a.spec.id: policy.control(a, agents, road, step_settings) for a in agents if not a.done}
        following = []
        for a in agents:
            if a.done:
                following.append(a)
                continue
            u = controls[a.spec.id]
            b = replace(a, x=a.x + a.spec.direction * u.speed * dt,
                        y=a.y + u.vy * dt, speed=u.speed, vy=u.vy,
                        mode=u.mode, committed=u.committed)
            b.path_length += math.hypot(b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y)
            if math.hypot(b.speed, b.vy) < c.stop_speed:
                b.waiting_time += dt
            b.peak_decel = max(a.peak_decel, (a.speed - b.speed) / dt)
            braking_infeasible_steps += int(u.braking_infeasible)
            following.append(b)
        for i, j in combinations(range(len(agents)), 2):
            a, b, an, bn = agents[i], agents[j], following[i], following[j]
            if a.done or b.done:
                continue
            contacts = []
            for bi, bni in pair_images(a, b, bn, road):
                contact = swept_contact(a, an, bi, bni)
                min_gap = min(min_gap, swept_separation(a, an, bi, bni))
                if contact is not None:
                    contacts.append(contact)
            if contacts:
                pair = tuple(sorted((a.spec.id, b.spec.id)))
                collisions.add(pair)
                events.append({"time": t + min(contacts) * dt, "type": "collision", "agents": pair})
            threshold = -(a.spec.length + b.spec.length) / 2 - c.longitudinal_clearance
            if (a.spec.direction != b.spec.direction and not contacts and
                relative_front(a, b, road) >= threshold > relative_front(an, bn, road)):
                pair_passages += 1
                events.append({"time": t + dt, "type": "pair_passage", "agents": [a.spec.id, b.spec.id]})
        for a, b in zip(agents, following):
            if a.done:
                continue
            gap = swept_wall_gap(road, a, b)
            min_wall = min(min_wall, gap)
            if gap < -1e-8:
                violations.add(a.spec.id)
                events.append({"time": t + dt, "type": "wall_violation", "agent": a.spec.id})
            if road.periodic:
                detector_crossings[b.spec.id] += abs(math.floor(b.x / road.length) - math.floor(a.x / road.length))
            crossed = (b.x - b.spec.length / 2 >= road.length if b.spec.direction == 1 else b.x + b.spec.length / 2 <= 0) if not road.periodic else False
            if crossed:
                # Count only when the trailing edge leaves. Retain final footprint in trace.
                b.done, b.mode = True, "DONE"
                boundary = road.length + b.spec.length / 2 if b.spec.direction == 1 else -b.spec.length / 2
                fraction = (boundary - a.x) / (b.x - a.x)
                b.exit_time = t + fraction * dt
                exit_times[b.spec.id] = b.exit_time
                events.append({"time": b.exit_time, "type": "exit", "agent": b.spec.id})
        t += dt
        agents = following
        if record and step % c.record_every == 0:
            trace.append(snapshot(t, agents))
        if collisions or violations:
            status = "collision" if collisions else "boundary_violation"
            break
        if all(a.done for a in agents):
            status = "completed"
            break
        live = [a for a in agents if not a.done]
        quiet = all(math.hypot(a.speed, a.vy) < c.stop_speed for a in live)
        quiet_time = quiet_time + dt if quiet else 0.0
        if quiet_time >= c.stall_time:
            status = classify_stall(scenario, agents)
            events.append({"time": t, "type": "stall", "classification": status})
            break
    if road.periodic and status == "timeout":
        status = "sustained"
    if record and (not trace or abs(trace[-1]["time"] - t) > 1e-7):
        trace.append(snapshot(t, agents))
    counts = {kind: sum(a.done and a.spec.kind == kind for a in agents) for kind in ("pedestrian", "vehicle")}
    total = {kind: sum(a.spec.kind == kind for a in agents) for kind in counts}
    finish = max(exit_times.values()) if status == "completed" else None
    metrics = {"status": status, "observation_time_s": t, "completion_time_s": finish,
               "pedestrians_passed": counts["pedestrian"], "vehicles_passed": counts["vehicle"],
               "pedestrians_total": total["pedestrian"], "vehicles_total": total["vehicle"],
               "collision_pairs": len(collisions), "boundary_violations": len(violations),
               "pair_passages": pair_passages,
               "detector_crossings": detector_crossings if road.periodic else None,
               "total_laps": sum(int(abs(a.x - start.x) / road.length) for a, start in zip(agents, scenario.agents)) if road.periodic else None,
               "longitudinal_distance_m": sum(abs(a.x - start.x) for a, start in zip(agents, scenario.agents)),
               "mean_longitudinal_speed_m_s": sum(abs(a.x - start.x) for a, start in zip(agents, scenario.agents)) / (t * len(agents)) if t else 0,
               "effective_speed_planned_horizon_m_s": sum(abs(a.x - start.x) for a, start in zip(agents, scenario.agents)) / (c.max_time * len(agents)) if road.periodic else None,
               "pair_passages_per_min_planned": 60 * pair_passages / c.max_time if road.periodic else None,
               "safety_margins_satisfied": not collisions and not violations and min_gap >= c.lateral_clearance - c.clearance_tolerance and min_wall >= c.wall_clearance - c.clearance_tolerance,
               "min_pair_gap_m": min_gap if math.isfinite(min_gap) else None,
               "min_wall_gap_m": min_wall,
               "speed_ceiling_unreachable_agent_steps": braking_infeasible_steps,
               "total_agent_waiting_s": sum(a.waiting_time for a in agents),
               "pedestrian_flow_per_min": 60 * counts["pedestrian"] / t if t else 0,
               "vehicle_flow_per_min": 60 * counts["vehicle"] / t if t else 0,
               "agents": [{"id": a.spec.id, "exit_time_s": a.exit_time,
                           "waiting_time_s": a.waiting_time, "path_length_m": a.path_length,
                           "peak_decel_m_s2": a.peak_decel} for a in agents]}
    return {"policy": {"name": getattr(policy, "name", type(policy).__name__),
                       "parameters": asdict(policy.parameters) if hasattr(policy, "parameters") else {}},
            "scenario": {"name": scenario.name, "seed": scenario.seed, "road": asdict(road),
                         "settings": asdict(c), "agents": [asdict(a.spec) for a in scenario.agents],
                         "initial_states": [asdict(a) for a in scenario.agents], "metadata": scenario.metadata},
            "feasibility": feasibility(scenario), "metrics": metrics, "events": events, "trace": trace}

pvsim/social_force.py

"""Uncalibrated Helbing-style baseline with common rectangular envelopes.

A and wall_A are accelerations. This is an adapted SFM, not a verbatim
implementation of the original circular-pedestrian 1995 model.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import math
from .policy import Control, ReciprocalPolicy, clip
from .topology import neighbor_images


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SocialForceParameters:
    A: float = 8.0
    B: float = 1.5
    wall_A: float = 3.0
    wall_B: float = 0.2
    anisotropy: float = 0.3

    def __post_init__(self):
        if min(self.A, self.B, self.wall_A, self.wall_B) <= 0 or not 0 <= self.anisotropy <= 1:
            raise ValueError("Invalid social-force parameters")


def repulsion(a, b):
    dx, dy = a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y
    distance = math.hypot(dx, dy)
    if distance < 1e-12:
        return -min(a.spec.width, b.spec.width), float(a.spec.direction), 0.0
    nx, ny = dx / distance, dy / distance
    hx, hy = (a.spec.length + b.spec.length) / 2, (a.spec.width + b.spec.width) / 2
    # SFM uses a centre-to-centre normal. A directional effective radius adapts
    # circular personal space to the common rectangle dimensions; it is NOT
    # the exact gradient of rectangle distance. Collision checks remain exact.
    radius = min(hx / max(abs(nx), 1e-12), hy / max(abs(ny), 1e-12))
    return distance - radius, nx, ny


class SocialForcePolicy:
    name = "social_force"

    def __init__(self, parameters=None, resolved_gate=False):
        self.parameters = parameters or SocialForceParameters()
        self.resolved_gate = resolved_gate

    def control(self, agent, agents, road, settings):
        p, q, c = agent.spec, self.parameters, settings
        desired_vy = clip((p.home_y - agent.y) / p.lateral_tau, -p.lateral_speed, p.lateral_speed)
        ax = (p.direction * p.desired_speed - agent.vx) / p.speed_tau
        ay = (desired_vy - agent.vy) / p.lateral_tau
        for other in neighbor_images(agent, agents, road):
            if (self.resolved_gate and p.direction != other.spec.direction and
                abs(agent.y - other.y) - (p.width + other.spec.width) / 2 >=
                c.lateral_clearance - c.clearance_tolerance):
                continue
            distance, nx, ny = repulsion(agent, other)
            cos_front = -p.direction * nx
            weight = q.anisotropy + (1 - q.anisotropy) * (1 + cos_front) / 2
            strength = q.A * math.exp(clip(-distance / q.B, -50, 10)) * weight
            ax += strength * nx
            ay += strength * ny
        width = road.min_width(agent.x - p.length / 2, agent.x + p.length / 2)
        lower = agent.y + width / 2 - p.width / 2
        upper = width / 2 - agent.y - p.width / 2
        if road.physical_walls:
            ay += q.wall_A * (math.exp(clip(-lower / q.wall_B, -50, 10)) - math.exp(clip(-upper / q.wall_B, -50, 10)))
        speed = clip(agent.speed + clip(p.direction * ax, -p.max_brake, p.max_accel) * c.dt, 0, p.desired_speed)
        vy = clip(agent.vy + clip(ay, -p.lateral_accel, p.lateral_accel) * c.dt, -p.lateral_speed, p.lateral_speed)
        if math.hypot(speed, vy) < c.stop_speed:
            mode = "WAIT"
        elif speed < agent.speed - 1e-6:
            mode = "BRAKE"
        elif abs(vy) > c.stop_speed:
            mode = "SIDESTEP"
        else:
            mode = "FREE"
        return Control(speed, vy, mode, False)


class GuidedSocialForcePolicy:
    """Ablation repair: keep SFM longitudinal force, add route choice + speed cap.

The lateral command is explicitly replaced by the shared rule planner; report
this as a hybrid, never as evidence that a pure SFM guarantees safe navigation.
"""
    name = "sfm_guided"

    def __init__(self, parameters=None, release_resolved=True):
        self.base = SocialForcePolicy(parameters, resolved_gate=release_resolved)
        self.parameters = self.base.parameters
        self.guide = ReciprocalPolicy()
        if not release_resolved:
            self.name = "sfm_guided_ungated"

    def control(self, agent, agents, road, settings):
        sf = self.base.control(agent, agents, road, settings)
        rule = self.guide.control(agent, agents, road, settings)
        return Control(min(sf.speed, rule.speed), rule.vy, rule.mode, rule.committed, rule.braking_infeasible)


def make_policy(name, parameters=None):
    if name == "reciprocal":
        return ReciprocalPolicy()
    if name == "social_force":
        return SocialForcePolicy(parameters)
    if name == "sfm_guided":
        return GuidedSocialForcePolicy(parameters)
    if name == "sfm_guided_ungated":
        return GuidedSocialForcePolicy(parameters, release_resolved=False)
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown policy: {name}")

pvsim/topology.py

"""Coordinate topology is independent from behavioral rules.

Ring means a periodic unrolled strip (s mod L, y), not a rigid-body curved-road
solver. The circular display maps its rectangular footprints to annular sectors.
"""
from dataclasses import replace


def neighbor_images(agent, agents, road):
    others = []
    for other in agents:
        if other.done or other.spec.id == agent.spec.id:
            continue
        if not road.periodic:
            others.append(other)
            continue
        if other.spec.direction == agent.spec.direction:
            front = (agent.spec.direction * (other.x - agent.x)) % road.length
            x = agent.x + agent.spec.direction * front
        else:
            delta = (other.x - agent.x + road.length / 2) % road.length - road.length / 2
            x = agent.x + delta
        others.append(replace(other, x=x))
    return others


def pair_images(a, b, bn, road):
    if not road.periodic:
        return [(b, bn)]
    nearest = round((a.x - b.x) / road.length)
    return [(replace(b, x=b.x + k * road.length), replace(bn, x=bn.x + k * road.length))
            for k in (nearest - 1, nearest, nearest + 1)]


def relative_front(a, b, road):
    delta = b.x - a.x
    if road.periodic:
        delta = (delta + road.length / 2) % road.length - road.length / 2
    return a.spec.direction * delta

tests/test_circle_crossing.py

import copy
from dataclasses import replace
import math
import unittest
from pvsim.domain import Settings
from pvsim.radial import circle_crossing, local_control
from pvsim.radial_geometry import swept_obb_contact, projected_gap, basis, dot
from pvsim.policy import ReciprocalPolicy
from pvsim.simulation import simulate
from pvsim.scenarios import load_scenario


class CircleCrossingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_alternating_inward_start_and_antipodal_goals(self):
        scene=circle_crossing(speed_jitter=0)
        self.assertEqual([a.spec.kind for a in scene.agents],['vehicle','pedestrian']*4)
        for a in scene.agents:
            self.assertAlmostEqual(math.hypot(a.x,a.y),12)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(dot((a.x,a.y),basis(scene.headings[a.spec.id])[0]),-12)
            self.assertEqual(scene.goals[a.spec.id],(-a.x,-a.y))

    def test_rotation_covariance(self):
        angle=.371
        a=simulate(circle_crossing(duration=2,rotation=0,speed_jitter=0))
        b=simulate(circle_crossing(duration=2,rotation=angle,speed_jitter=0))
        for fa,fb in zip(a['trace'],b['trace']):
            for x,y in zip(fa['agents'],fb['agents']):
                self.assertAlmostEqual(y['x'],x['x']*math.cos(angle)-x['y']*math.sin(angle),places=5)
                self.assertAlmostEqual(y['y'],x['x']*math.sin(angle)+x['y']*math.cos(angle),places=5)
                self.assertAlmostEqual(x['speed'],y['speed'],places=5)

    def test_type_and_mass_do_not_change_policy(self):
        a=circle_crossing(duration=2)
        b=copy.deepcopy(a)
        for agent in b.agents:agent.spec=replace(agent.spec,kind='unknown',mass=12345)
        self.assertEqual(simulate(a)['trace'],simulate(b)['trace'])

    def test_one_pair_arrives_without_teleporting(self):
        scene=circle_crossing(pairs=1,settings=Settings(record_every=1))
        result=simulate(scene)
        self.assertEqual(result['metrics']['status'],'completed')
        self.assertEqual(result['metrics']['arrived_total'],2)
        self.assertEqual(result['metrics']['collision_pairs'],0)
        previous=result['trace'][0]
        for frame in result['trace'][1:]:
            dt=frame['time']-previous['time']
            for old,new,spec in zip(previous['agents'],frame['agents'],scene.agents):
                self.assertLessEqual(new['speed']-old['speed'],spec.spec.max_accel*dt+2e-6)
                self.assertLessEqual(old['speed']-new['speed'],spec.spec.max_brake*dt+2e-6)
                self.assertLessEqual(abs(new['vy']-old['vy']),spec.spec.lateral_accel*dt+2e-6)
                e,n=basis(scene.headings[new['id']])
                self.assertAlmostEqual(new['x']-old['x'],(new['speed']*e[0]+new['vy']*n[0])*dt,places=5)
                self.assertAlmostEqual(new['y']-old['y'],(new['speed']*e[1]+new['vy']*n[1])*dt,places=5)
                if new['done']:self.assertEqual((new['speed'],new['vy']),(0,0))
            previous=frame

    def test_fixed_vehicle_has_no_lateral_drift(self):
        scene=circle_crossing(duration=3)
        result=simulate(scene)
        for frame in result['trace']:
            for a in frame['agents']:
                if a['id'].startswith('V'):
                    self.assertAlmostEqual(dot((a['x'],a['y']),basis(scene.headings[a['id']])[1]),0,places=5)

    def test_crossing_obb_collision_between_nonoverlapping_endpoints(self):
        scene=circle_crossing(pairs=1)
        a,b=scene.agents
        a,b=replace(a,x=-6,y=0),replace(b,x=0,y=-6)
        an,bn=replace(a,x=6),replace(b,y=6)
        self.assertGreater(projected_gap(a,b,0,math.pi/2),0)
        self.assertGreater(projected_gap(an,bn,0,math.pi/2),0)
        self.assertIsNotNone(swept_obb_contact(a,an,b,bn,0,math.pi/2))

    def test_arrived_body_remains_an_obstacle(self):
        scene=circle_crossing(pairs=1,speed_jitter=0)
        a,b=scene.agents
        # Put a stopped, already-arrived body directly in front of a vehicle.
        a=replace(a,x=5,y=0,speed=1)
        b=replace(b,x=1,y=0,speed=0,vy=0,done=True)
        scene.agents=[a,b]
        u=local_control(a,scene.agents,scene,ReciprocalPolicy(),scene.settings)
        free=local_control(a,[a],scene,ReciprocalPolicy(),scene.settings)
        self.assertLess(u.speed,free.speed)

    def test_dt_convergence_single_pair(self):
        results=[simulate(circle_crossing(pairs=1,settings=Settings(dt=dt)),record=False)['metrics'] for dt in (.05,.025)]
        self.assertEqual([r['status'] for r in results],['completed','completed'])
        self.assertLess(abs(results[0]['completion_time_s']-results[1]['completion_time_s']),.5)


if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

tests/test_ring_and_sfm.py

import copy
from dataclasses import replace
import unittest
from pvsim.scenarios import periodic_corridor as ring, corridor
from pvsim.simulation import simulate, validate
from pvsim.domain import Settings
from pvsim.topology import pair_images, neighbor_images
from pvsim.geometry import swept_contact
from pvsim.social_force import SocialForcePolicy, GuidedSocialForcePolicy, SocialForceParameters


class RingAndSFMTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_ring_repeated_encounters_and_no_exits(self):
        r = simulate(ring(duration=120), record=False)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["status"], "sustained")
        self.assertGreater(r["metrics"]["pair_passages"], 6)
        self.assertGreater(r["metrics"]["total_laps"], 0)
        self.assertIsNone(r["metrics"]["completion_time_s"])
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["pedestrians_passed"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["collision_pairs"], 0)

    def test_seam_neighbor_and_continuous_collision(self):
        case = ring(pedestrians=1, vehicles=1)
        a, b = case.agents
        a, b = replace(a, x=75, y=0), replace(b, x=5, y=0)
        bi = neighbor_images(a, [a, b], case.road)[0]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(bi.x, 85)
        an, bn = replace(a, x=85), replace(b, x=-5)
        self.assertTrue(any(swept_contact(a, an, i, j) is not None for i, j in pair_images(a, b, bn, case.road)))

    def test_seam_initial_overlap_rejected(self):
        case = ring(pedestrians=1, vehicles=1)
        case.agents[0].x, case.agents[1].x = 79.5, .5
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            validate(case)

    def test_sfm_is_type_and_mass_invariant(self):
        case = corridor(pedestrians=1, vehicles=1, settings=Settings(max_time=3))
        other = copy.deepcopy(case)
        for a in other.agents:
            a.spec = replace(a.spec, kind="unlabeled", mass=99999)
        self.assertEqual(simulate(case, SocialForcePolicy())["trace"], simulate(other, SocialForcePolicy())["trace"])

    def test_hybrid_releases_resolved_conflict(self):
        result = simulate(corridor(width=3.2), GuidedSocialForcePolicy(), record=False)
        self.assertEqual(result["metrics"]["status"], "completed")
        self.assertEqual(result["metrics"]["collision_pairs"], 0)

    def test_short_range_sfm_failure_is_reported(self):
        result = simulate(corridor(width=3.2), SocialForcePolicy(SocialForceParameters(A=4, B=.8)), record=False)
        self.assertIn(result["metrics"]["status"], ("collision", "boundary_violation"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

tests/test_simulation.py

import copy
from dataclasses import replace
import unittest
from pvsim import Settings, Road, Scenario
from pvsim.scenarios import corridor
from pvsim.simulation import simulate, validate
from pvsim.geometry import swept_contact, swept_separation, swept_wall_gap
from pvsim.policy import Control, ReciprocalPolicy


class SimulationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_safe_threshold_and_no_contact(self):
        r = simulate(corridor(width=2.8), record=False)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["status"], "completed")
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["collision_pairs"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["boundary_violations"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["pedestrians_passed"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["vehicles_passed"], 2)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(r["metrics"]["min_pair_gap_m"], 0.2 - 1e-5)
        for a in r["metrics"]["agents"]:
            self.assertIsNotNone(a["exit_time_s"])

    def test_physical_fits_but_safety_does_not(self):
        r = simulate(corridor(width=2.6), record=False)
        self.assertTrue(r["feasibility"]["physical_cross_section_fits"])
        self.assertFalse(r["feasibility"]["safe_cross_section_fits"])
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["status"], "waiting_clearance")
        self.assertIsNone(r["metrics"]["completion_time_s"])
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["collision_pairs"], 0)

    def test_fixed_centered_pose_and_small_vehicle_shift(self):
        stopped = simulate(corridor(width=2.9, centered_vehicle=True), record=False)
        passed = simulate(corridor(width=2.9, centered_vehicle=True, vehicle_lateral_speed=.2), record=False)
        self.assertEqual(stopped["metrics"]["status"], "waiting_alignment")
        self.assertEqual(passed["metrics"]["status"], "completed")

    def test_narrowing_waits_and_wider_narrowing_passes(self):
        self.assertEqual(simulate(corridor(width=2.6, bottleneck=True), record=False)["metrics"]["status"], "waiting_clearance")
        self.assertEqual(simulate(corridor(width=3.2, bottleneck=True), record=False)["metrics"]["status"], "completed")

    def test_type_labels_and_mass_do_not_change_motion(self):
        base = corridor(width=3.0, pedestrians=1, vehicles=1)
        changed = copy.deepcopy(base)
        for a in changed.agents:
            a.spec = replace(a.spec, kind="other", mass=a.spec.mass * 100)
        self.assertEqual(simulate(base)["trace"], simulate(changed)["trace"])

    def test_order_independence_synchronous_updates(self):
        base = corridor()
        reversed_scenario = copy.deepcopy(base)
        reversed_scenario.agents.reverse()
        r1, r2 = simulate(base), simulate(reversed_scenario)
        for frame1, frame2 in zip(r1["trace"], r2["trace"]):
            self.assertEqual(sorted(frame1["agents"], key=lambda a: a["id"]),
                             sorted(frame2["agents"], key=lambda a: a["id"]))

    def test_limits_and_fixed_vehicle_lane(self):
        scenario = corridor(settings=Settings(record_every=1))
        result = simulate(scenario)
        specs = {a.spec.id: a.spec for a in scenario.agents}
        for previous, current in zip(result["trace"], result["trace"][1:]):
            dt = current["time"] - previous["time"]
            for a, b in zip(previous["agents"], current["agents"]):
                p = specs[a["id"]]
                self.assertLessEqual((b["speed"] - a["speed"]) / dt, p.max_accel + 1e-4)
                self.assertGreaterEqual((b["speed"] - a["speed"]) / dt, -p.max_brake - 1e-4)
                self.assertLessEqual(abs(b["vy"] - a["vy"]) / dt, p.lateral_accel + 1e-4)
                if p.lateral_speed == 0:
                    self.assertEqual(a["y"], b["y"])

    def test_swept_detection_catches_tunneling(self):
        a, b = corridor(pedestrians=1, vehicles=1).agents
        a0, a1 = replace(a, x=10, y=0), replace(a, x=30, y=0)
        b0, b1 = replace(b, x=30, y=0), replace(b, x=10, y=0)
        self.assertIsNotNone(swept_contact(a0, a1, b0, b1))
        self.assertEqual(swept_separation(a0, a1, b0, b1), 0)
        far = replace(b0, y=10)
        self.assertIsNone(swept_contact(a0, a1, far, far))

    def test_swept_wall_checks_interior_bottleneck(self):
        a = corridor(pedestrians=1, vehicles=1).agents[0]
        road = Road(60, ((0, 5), (20, 5), (25, 2), (30, 5), (60, 5)))
        start, end = replace(a, x=10, y=1), replace(a, x=40, y=1)
        self.assertLess(swept_wall_gap(road, start, end), 0)

    def test_unsafe_initial_speed_is_not_hidden_by_position_projection(self):
        r = simulate(corridor(width=2.6, pedestrians=1, vehicles=1,
                              vehicle_speed=12, initial_gap=5, speed_jitter=0))
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["status"], "collision")
        self.assertGreater(r["metrics"]["speed_ceiling_unreachable_agent_steps"], 0)

    def test_invalid_overlap_rejected(self):
        bad = corridor(pedestrians=1, vehicles=1)
        bad.agents[1].x = bad.agents[0].x
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            validate(bad)

    def test_timeout_is_not_completion_or_stall(self):
        r = simulate(corridor(settings=Settings(max_time=.1)), record=False)
        self.assertEqual(r["metrics"]["status"], "timeout")
        self.assertIsNone(r["metrics"]["completion_time_s"])

    def test_repeatable_and_no_mutation(self):
        case = corridor(seed=42)
        original = copy.deepcopy(case)
        self.assertEqual(simulate(case)["metrics"], simulate(case)["metrics"])
        self.assertEqual(case, original)

    def test_dt_convergence(self):
        times = [simulate(corridor(width=2.9, settings=Settings(dt=dt)), record=False)["metrics"]["completion_time_s"]
                 for dt in (.1, .05, .025)]
        self.assertLess(max(times) - min(times), .7)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
